                                               specified person

                                               制或與該特定人士直接或間接受共同控制的任何其他人士

                                                                                                                          釋義




“Company” or “our Company”                Shanghai REFIRE Group Limited (上海重塑能源集團股份有限公司), a joint stock
                                          company with limited liability incorporated in the PRC, the predecessor of which
                                          was Shanghai REFIRE Group Ltd. (上海重塑能源集團有限公司) (formerly known as
                                          Hangzhou REFIRE Technology Co., Ltd. (重塑能源科技(杭州)有限公司)), a limited
                                          liability company established in the PRC on September 18, 2015, and if the context
                                          requires, includes its predecessor
「本公司」                                 指   上海重塑能源集團股份有限公司，一家在中國註冊成立的股份有限公司，其前身
                                          為上海重塑能源集團有限公司（前稱重塑能源科技（杭州）有限公司），一家於2015
                                          年9月18日在中國成立的有限責任公司，如文意所指，包括其前身


“Components for Fuel Cell System”         components self-developed and produced by our Group, including fuel cell stacks,
                                          MEAs, bipolar plates, hydrogen circulation systems, DC-to-DC boost converters, etc.
「燃料電池系統零部件」                           指   本集團自主研發生產的零部件，包括燃料電池電堆、膜電極、雙極板、氫循環系
                                          統、直流升壓轉換器等


“Components for Fuel Cell Vehicles”       components sold to our customers for producing fuel cell vehicles, including hydrogen
                                          supply systems, fuel cell engine accessories, energy storage systems, etc.
「燃料電池汽車零部件」                           指   向客戶銷售的用於生產燃料電池汽車的零部件，包括供氫系統、燃料電池發動機
                                          配件、儲能系統等

管理層討論及分析


I. BUSINESS OVERVIEW                                                                I. 業務概覽
Market and Industry Review                                                          市場及行業回顧
In 2024, China’s top-level policy design for hydrogen energy became                 2024 年，中國氫能頂層政策設計趨於完善，中
more comprehensive. The Government Work Report (《政府工作報告》),                          華人民共和國國務院發表的《政府工作報告》中
as promulgated by the State Council of the People’s Republic of China,              提出加快前沿新興氫能產業發展；
                                                                                                  《關於加快推
proposed accelerating the development of emerging hydrogen energy                   動製造業綠色化發展的指導意見》、《加快經濟
industries. The “Guiding Opinions on Accelerating Green Development in              社會發展全面綠色轉型的意見》中提出推進氫
Manufacturing” and the “Guidance on Accelerating the Comprehensive                  能「製儲輸用」全鏈條發展；2024年11月《中華人
Green Transformation of Economic and Social Development” proposed to                民共和國能源法》正式將氫能列入能源範疇，與
promote the entire hydrogen energy supply chain, covering production,               石油、煤炭、天然氣、核電、水能等並列作為
storage, transportation, and utilization. In November 2024, the Energy Law          能源進行管理。氫能在國家能源體系和綠色發
of the People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國能源法》) officially                         展中的戰略地位得到明確。在交通、工業、建
recognized hydrogen as an energy source, placing it alongside oil, coal,            築、電力等領域，氫能被認為是實現綠色轉型
natural gas, nuclear power, and hydropower for regulatory purposes. This            發展的重要路徑。在世界能源轉型和中國實現
decision clarified hydrogen’s strategic role within the national energy             碳達峰、碳中和目標過程中，氫能的關鍵作用
system and its importance for green development. Hydrogen is seen as a              已成為全球共識。在政策引導和市場需求的驅
crucial pathway for achieving green transformation across multiple sectors,         動下，以重卡為代表的氫能應用場景得到商業
including transportation, industry, construction, and power generation.             化驗證和突破，燃料電池汽車產業技術發展逐
Globally, hydrogen’s significant role in energy transition, as well as China’s      漸成熟。大功率燃料電池系統實現批量應用，
goals of reaching peak carbon emissions and achieving carbon neutrality, has        打通了重載高速長距離運輸場景。重卡應用形
gained widespread acknowledgment. Driven by policy support and market               成的基礎使氫能及燃料電池多元應用具備了條
demand, hydrogen applications, especially in heavy-duty trucks, have seen           件，在氫能離網超充、物料搬運、綠色港口、
commercial validation and breakthroughs. Fuel cell vehicle technology has           綠色礦山、石油裝備、鐵路運輸等廣泛的領
matured, with high-power fuel cell systems now being deployed on a large            域，已形成應用示範。綠氫製儲輸用一體化項
scale, facilitating heavy-load, high-speed and long-distance transportation.        目取得進展，可再生能源發電、電解水製氫、
The foundational work in heavy-duty truck applications has paved the way            綠氫及燃料電池下游消納相結合的氫電產業生
for various uses of hydrogen and fuel cells. Demonstrations of hydrogen             態初步形成。
applications are emerging in areas such as off-grid ultra-fast charging,
material handling, green ports, green mining, oil equipment, and railway
transportation. Integrated projects focusing on green hydrogen production,
storage, transportation, and utilization are making progress, creating an initial
hydrogen-electricity industry ecosystem that combines renewable energy
generation, electrolysis for hydrogen production, and the downstream
consumption of green hydrogen and fuel cells.

                                                                                  MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
                                                                                                    管理層討論及分析




In 2024, the hydrogen energy industry underwent a significant transition from     2024 年氫能產業已進入到政策驅動向市場驅動
being policy-driven to market-driven growth. The application of hydrogen          的發展窗口期，氫能應用在從中國示範城市群
energy expanded from demonstration city clusters to non-demonstration             向非示範城市群、從商用車場景到其他場景、
city clusters in China, from commercial vehicle scenarios to other scenarios,     從中國國內市場到海外市場的發展過程中，帶
and from the domestic Chinese market to international markets, which              來了一系列機遇和挑戰。重塑能源在2024 年
presented a series of opportunities and challenges. Throughout 2024, we           取得了穩健的發展。我們保持了車用燃料電池
achieved steady growth. We maintained the stability and competitive               核心業務的穩定性和領先優勢，重卡裝機量行
advantage of our core business in automotive fuel cells, leading the industry     業領先；海外業務實現大幅增長；業務創新取

Foreign Exchange Risk                                                            外匯風險
While the Group primarily operates in the PRC and transactions are primarily     儘管本集團主要在中國開展業務，交易主要以
denominated and settled in Renminbi, the Group also has business                 人民幣計值及結算，但本集團亦於其他海外市
operations in other overseas markets. As such, the Group is exposed to           場經營業務。因此，本集團面臨來自人民幣與
foreign currency risk arising from fluctuations in exchange rates between        本集團開展有關業務所涉及的其他貨幣匯率的
the RMB and other currencies relating to the Group’s business. Further, the      波動所產生的外匯風險。此外，本集團面臨的
Group is subject to foreign currency risk attributable to the bank balances      外匯風險源自以人民幣以外的貨幣計值的銀行
that are denominated in currencies other than RMB. The Group seeks to limit      結餘。本集團尋求通過盡量降低外幣淨頭寸來
the exposure to foreign currency risk by minimizing its net foreign currency     限制所面臨的外匯風險。截至 2024 年12 月31
position. The Group did not enter into any hedging transactions in respect of    日，本集團並無就外匯風險訂立任何對沖交
foreign currency risk as of December 31, 2024. The Directors expect that the     易。董事預期人民幣匯率的波動將不會對本集

chain in the field of fuel cells and hydrogen production systems, we provide   需求提供製氫及終端應用一站式解決方案，推

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                                         股息政策
As the Company intends to retain all of its future earnings to finance the              由於本公司擬保留所有未來利潤以為本公司業
development and growth of the Company’s business, it has not yet adopted a              務的發展及增長提供資金，其尚未採納宣派或
dividend policy to declare or pay any dividends. The declaration and payment of         派付任何股息的股息政策。日後任何股息宣派
any dividends in the future will be determined by our Board and subject to the          及派付將由董事會釐定並須遵守公司章程及《中
Articles of Association and the Company Law of the People’s Republic of China           華人民共和國公司法》，且將取決於多種因素，
(《中華人民共和國公司法》), and will depend on a number of factors, including                       包括我們的財務表現、業務經營、資本要求及
our financial performance and business operation, capital requirements and              合約限制。股息僅可自可用於合法分派的利潤
contractual restrictions. No dividend shall be declared or payable except out of        及儲備中宣派或派付。誠如中國法律顧問確
our profits and reserves lawfully available for distribution. As confirmed by our       認，根據中國法律，本公司日後獲得的任何利
PRC legal adviser, according to the PRC laws, any future net profits obtained by        潤淨額必須首先用於彌補其累計過往虧損，其
the Company must first be used to make up for its accumulated prior losses,             後本公司須將其利潤淨額的至少10%分配至法定
after which the Company is obliged to allocate at least 10% of its net profits to       公積金，直至法定公積金超過本公司註冊資本
the statutory capital reserve until the cumulative amount of the statutory capital      50%為止。因此，本公司將僅能於(i)已經彌補其
reserve exceeds 50% of the Company’ registered capital. Therefore, the Company          過往財政年度的全部過往累計虧損；及(ii)本公
will only be able to declare dividends after (i) all its historical accumulated         司已分配充足利潤淨額至上述法定公積金，方
losses from prior fiscal years have been made up for; and (ii) the Company has          能宣派股息。董事會將定期審閱本公司狀況並
allocated sufficient net profits to the statutory capital reserve as described above.   考慮於適當時候採納股息政策。
The Board will regularly review the Company’ status and consider adopting a
dividend policy if and when appropriate.


The Board did not recommend the payment of a final dividend for the year                董事會並不建議就截至2024 年12 月31 日止年度
ended December 31, 2024.                                                                派付末期股息。

are generally remunerated by way of a fixed salary. Our Group utilises an       僱員一般以收取固定薪金的方式獲得薪酬。本
appraisal system for our employees and considers the appraisal results of       集團為僱員採用一套評核系統，並於進行薪金

decisions and determining the amount of bonuses. We also maintain social        別僱員的評核結果。我們亦按照中國適用法律
insurance coverage for our employees in accordance with the applicable PRC      及地方機關的規定為僱員購買社會保險。
laws and the requirements of the local authorities.

committee was set up to review the Company’s emolument policy and                     檢討本公司的薪酬政策以及本公司全體董事及
structure for all remuneration of the Directors and senior management of              高級管理層的薪酬架構，其中會慮及本公司的
the Company, having regard to the Company’s operating results, individual             經營業績、董事及高級管理層的個人表現及可

The Incentive Scheme has been formulated to further establish and                本激勵計劃的目的是進一步建立、健全公司長
improve the Company's long-term incentive mechanism, attract and retain          效激勵機制，吸引和保留優秀人才，充分調動

and effectively align Shareholders' interests, the Company's interests and       益和核心團隊個人利益結合在一起，使各方共

make joint efforts for the Company's long-term development.

Proposed Dissolution of Supervisory Committee and Proposed                       建議解散監事會及建議修訂公司章程
Amendments to Articles of Association
Pursuant to the amendments to the Company Law of the People’s Republic           根據於2024年7月1日起施行的《中華人民共和國
of China (《中華人民共和國公司法》) effective on July 1, 2024 (the “New                      公司法》修正案（「新《公司法》」），股份有限公
Company Law”), a joint stock limited company may, in accordance with its         司可以按照公司章程的規定設置由董事組成的
Articles of Association, instead of establishing a Supervisory Committee,        審計委員會，行使新《公司法》規定的監事會的
establish an Audit Committee which comprises Directors and discharges the        職權，不設監事會。於2025年4月17日，鑒於新
duties of the Supervisory Committee as prescribed under the New Company          《公司法》的規定，董事會決議並提議解散監事
Law. On April 17, 2025, in light of the New Company Law, the Board resolved      會（「建議解散」），該事項完成後監事會的職權
and proposed to dissolve the Supervisory Committee (the “Proposed                將由董事會審計委員會行使。
Dissolution”), following which the duties of the Supervisory Committee will
be discharged by the Audit Committee of the Board.

perform his/her responsibilities to the Company, and whether the Director is    出的貢獻，以及董事是否付出足夠的時間履行
spending sufficient time performing them.                                       職責。

individual. Mr. LIN Qi is the chairman of the Board and the chief executive      琦先生為本公司董事長兼首席執行官。林先生
officer of the Company. With considerable experience in the fuel cell industry   於燃料電池行業擁有豐富經驗，自本集團成立

officer are both performed by Mr. Lin, which constitutes a deviation from        段，但董事會認為，董事長與首席執行官的角
paragraph C.2.1 of the CG Code, the Board considers that vesting the roles       色均由林先生擔任對本集團管理有利。董事會
of the chairman of the Board and the chief executive officer both in Mr.         及 高 級 管 理 層（ 由 經 驗 豐 富 的 人 士 組 成 ）的 運

and authority is ensured by the operation of the Board and the senior            事、一名非執行董事及三名獨立非執行董事組

business objectives, and can only provide reasonable but not absolute             並無重大失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對保
assurance against material misstatement or loss.                                  證。

effective risk management and internal control systems.

Revenue Recognition
收入確認

consolidated financial statements that are free from material misstatement,       必需的內部控制負責。
whether due to fraud or error.

extremely rare circumstances, we determine that a matter should not be          期在我們報告中溝通某事項造成的負面後果超
communicated in our report because the adverse consequences of doing so         過產生的公眾利益，我們決定不應在報告中溝
would reasonably be expected to outweigh the public interest benefits of        通該事項。
such communication.

report is Ho Wai Ling.                                                          惠玲。

1. CORPORATE AND GROUP INFORMATION                                                           1. 公司及集團資料
  Shanghai REFIRE Group Limited is a limited liability company                                     上海重塑能源集團股份有限公司為一家於
  incorporated in Shanghai on September 18, 2015. The registered office                            2015年9月18日在上海註冊成立的有限責任
  of the Company is located at Room 1004, 1/F, Unit 1, 1555 Jingyuan                               公司。本公司註冊辦事處位於中華人民共
  Road, Jiading District, Shanghai, the People’s Republic of China (the                            和國（「中國」）上海市嘉定區靖遠路1555號1
  “PRC”). On 11 September 2020, the Company was converted into a                                   幢1層1004室。於2020年9月11日，本公司
  joint stock company with limited liability. On December 6, 2024, the                             改制為股份有限公司。於2024年12月6日，
  Company’s shares were listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange                             本公司的股份於香港聯合交易所有限公司
  of Hong Kong Limited.                                                                            主板上市。

1. CORPORATE AND GROUP INFORMATION                                                     1. 公司及集團資料（續）

  Information about subsidiaries (continued)                                            有關附屬公司的資料（續）
  The English names of all group companies registered in the PRC                        所有於中國註冊的集團公司的英文名稱乃
  represent the best efforts made by the management of the Company                      由本公司管理層盡最大努力對該等公司中
  to translate the Chinese names of these companies as they do not have                 文名稱翻譯所得，乃因其並無正式英文名

2. ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                                            2. 會計政策（續）
  2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                             2.4 重大會計政策
     Investments in associates and a joint venture                                於聯營公司及一家合營企業的投
                                                                                  資
     An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long-term interest        聯營公司指本集團擁有一般不少於20%
     of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting rights and over          的投票權的長期權益且對其具有重大
     which it has significant influence. Significant influence is the power       影響的實體。重大影響是指參與投資
     to participate in the financial and operating policy decisions of the        對象的財務及經營政策決策的權力，
     investee, but is not control or joint control over those policies.           但並非對該等政策的控制或共同控制。


     A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby the parties           合營企業指一種合營安排，對安排擁
     that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the                有共同控制權的訂約方據此對合營企
     net assets of the joint ventures. Joint control is the contractually         業的資產淨值擁有權利。共同控制是
     agreed sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists only               指按照相關約定對某項安排所共有的
     when decisions about the relevant activities require the unanimous           控制，並且該安排的相關活動必須經
     consent of the parties sharing control.                                      過分享控制權的參與方一致同意後才
                                                                                  能決策。


     The Group’s investments in associates and a joint venture are stated         本集團於聯營公司及一家合營企業的
     in the consolidated statement of financial position at the Group’s           投資乃根據權益會計法按本集團所佔
     share of net assets under the equity method of accounting, less              資產淨值減任何減值損失計入綜合財
     any impairment losses. Adjustments are made to bring into line               務狀況表。對於可能存在的任何不同
     any dissimilar accounting policies that may exist. The Group’s share         會計政策，均會作出調整使其一致。
     of the post-acquisition results and other comprehensive income               本集團應佔聯營公司和合營企業的收
     of associates and joint ventures is included in the consolidated             購後業績和其他全面收益的份額分別

       Fair value measurement                                                       公允價值計量
       The Group measures its equity investments and private equity                 本集團於各報告期末以公允價值計量
       funds at fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair value is       其股權投資及私募股權基金。公允價
       the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer        值是指市場參與者於計量日發生的有
       a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants at         序交易中，出售一項資產所能收到或
       the measurement date. The fair value measurement is based on                 轉移一項負債所需支付的價格。公允

       the liability takes place either in the principal market for the asset       交易於資產或負債主要市場或（在無主
       or liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in the most           要市場情況下）最有利市場中進行。主
       advantageous market for the asset or liability. The principal or the         要市場或最有利市場必須是本集團能
       most advantageous market must be accessible by the Group. The                夠進入的市場。計量資產和負債公允

       that market participants would use when pricing the asset or                 負債定價時為實現其經濟利益最大化
       liability, assuming that market participants act in their economic           所使用的假設。
       best interest.

     Fair value measurement (continued)                                             公允價值計量（續）
     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account           非金融資產的公允價值計量考慮市場
     a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by                參與者將該資產用於最佳用途產生經
     using the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another        濟利益的能力，或者將該資產出售給
     market participant that would use the asset in its highest and best            能夠用於最佳用途的其他市場參與者
     use.                                                                           產生經濟利益的能力。


     The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the                本集團採用在有關情況下適用並且有
     circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to measure           足夠數據計量公允價值的估值技術，
     fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and               並盡可能多地使用相關可觀察輸入
     minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                                     值，盡可能少地使用不可觀察輸入值。


     All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or disclosed       所有其公允價值在財務報表中計量或
     in the financial statements are categorised within the fair value              披露的資產和負債，均根據對公允價
     hierarchy, described as follows, based on the lowest level input that          值計量整體具有重大意義的最低層級
     is significant to the fair value measurement as a whole:                       的輸入值，在下述公允價值層級內分
                                                                                    類：


     Level 1   –    based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active                   第一層級 －          根據同類資產或負債在
                    markets for identical assets or liabilities                                     活躍市場上的報價（未
                                                                                                    經調整）
     Level 2   –    based on valuation techniques for which the                     第二層級 －          根據對公允價值計量具
                    lowest level input that is significant to the fair                              有重大意義的直接或間
                    value measurement is observable, either directly or                             接可觀察最低層級的輸
                    indirectly                                                                      入值的估值方法
     Level 3   –    based on valuation techniques for which the                     第三層級 －          根據對公允價值計量具
                    lowest level input that is significant to the fair value                        有重大意義的不可觀察
                    measurement is unobservable                                                     最低層級的輸入值的估
                                                                                                    值方法


     For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the financial                對於持續在財務報表確認的資產和負
     statements on a recurring basis, the Group determines whether                  債，本集團在各報告期末通過重新評
     transfers have occurred between levels in the hierarchy by                     估分類（根據對整體公允價值計量具有
     reassessing categorisation (based on the lowest level input that is            重大意義的最低層級的輸入值）確定各
     significant to the fair value measurement as a whole) at the end of            層級之間是否存在轉移。
     each reporting period.

     financial year end.                                                          末進行檢討及調整（如適用）。

     Leases                                                                       租賃
     The Group assesses at contract inception whether a contract is, or           在合約開始日，本集團評估合約是否
     contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract       為租賃或者包含租賃，如果合約中一
     conveys the right to control the use of an identified asset for a            方讓渡了在一定期間內控制一項已識
     period of time in exchange for consideration.                                別資產使用的權利以換取代價，則該
                                                                                  合約為租賃或者包含租賃。


     Group as a lessee                                                            本集團作為承租人
     The Group applies a single recognition and measurement approach              本集團對所有租賃採用單一確認和計
     for all leases, except for short-term leases and leases of low-value         量方法，但短期租賃和低價值資產租
     assets. The Group recognises lease liabilities to make lease                 賃除外。本集團確認支付租賃付款額
     payments and right-of-use assets representing the right to use the           的租賃負債和代表使用標的資產權利
     underlying assets.                                                           的使用權資產。


     (a) Right-of-use assets                                                      (a) 使用權資產

         Right-of-use assets are recognised at the commencement                        本集團於租賃開始日（即標的資產
         date of the lease (that is the date the underlying asset is                   開始可供使用之日）確認使用權資
         available for use). Right-of-use assets are measured at cost,                 產。使用權資產以成本減累計折舊
         less accumulated depreciation and any impairment losses, and                  及任何減值損失計量，並就重新計
         adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities. The cost                 量租賃負債作出調整。使用權資產
         of right-of-use assets includes the amount of lease liabilities               的成本包括已確認的租賃負債金
         recognised, initial direct costs incurred, and lease payments                 額、已發生的初始直接成本以及在
         made at or before the commencement date less any lease                        租賃期開始日或之前支付的租賃付
         incentives received. Right-of-use assets are depreciated on a                 款額扣除已享受的租賃激勵相關金
         straight-line basis over the shorter of the lease terms and the               額。使用權資產按直線法在租賃期
         estimated useful lives of the assets as follows:                              與資產估計可使用年期兩者孰短的
                                                                                       期間內計提折舊：


         Leasehold land                                          50 years              租賃土地                       50年
         Plant and properties                                2 to 8 years              廠房及物業                     2至8年


         If ownership of the leased asset transfers to the Group by the                倘租賃資產的所有權在租賃期結束
         end of the lease term or the cost reflects the exercise of a                  時轉移至本集團或成本反映行使了
         purchase option, depreciation is calculated using the estimated               購買選擇權，則使用資產按估計可
         useful life of the asset.                                                     使用年期計算折舊。

       Investments and other financial assets (continued)                          投資及其他金融資產（續）
       Initial recognition and measurement (continued)                             初始確認及計量（續）
       In order for a financial asset to be classified and measured at             為使金融資產按攤銷成本或按公允價
       amortised cost or fair value through other comprehensive income,            值計入其他全面收益進行分類及計
       it needs to give rise to cash flows that are solely payments of             量，需產生純粹為支付本金及未償還
       principal and interest (“SPPI”) on the principal amount outstanding.        本金利息（「純粹為支付本金及利息」）
       Financial assets with cash flows that are not SPPI are classified and       的現金流量。現金流量並非純粹為支
       measured at fair value through profit or loss, irrespective of the          付本金及利息的金融資產，不論其業
       business model.                                                             務模式如何，均按公允價值計入損益
                                                                                   分類及計量。


       The Group’s business model for managing financial assets refers to          本集團管理金融資產的業務模式是指
       how it manages its financial assets in order to generate cash flows.        本集團如何管理其金融資產以產生現
       The business model determines whether cash flows will result                金流量。業務模式可以是以收取合約
       from collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial assets,       現金流量為目標，出售金融資產為目
       or both. Financial assets classified and measured at amortised              標或二者兼顧。持有被分類為按攤銷
       cost are held within a business model with the objective to hold            成本計量的金融資產的業務模式是以
       financial assets in order to collect contractual cash flows, while          收取合約現金流量為目標，持有被分
       financial assets classified and measured at fair value through              類為按公允價值計入其他全面收益的
       other comprehensive income are held within a business model                 金融資產的業務模式是既以收取合約
       with the objective of both holding to collect contractual cash              現金流量又以出售金融資產為目標。
       flows and selling. Financial assets which are not held within the           未被上述業務模式所持有的則被分類
       aforementioned business models are classified and measured at fair          為按公允價值計入損益的金融資產。
       value through profit or loss.


       Purchases or sales of financial assets that require delivery of assets      買賣金融資產是指在法規或通行慣例
       within the period generally established by regulation or convention         一般規定的期限內在交易市場交付資
       in the marketplace are recognised on the trade date, that is, the           產，並於交易日（即本集團承諾買入或
       date that the Group commits to purchase or sell the asset.                  賣出資產的日期）確認。

         substantially all the risks and rewards of the asset, but has                   集團既未轉移也未保留資產的絕大

2. ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                                               2. 會計政策（續）
   2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                                   2.4 重大會計政策（續）
       Impairment of financial assets                                               金融資產減值
       The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses                 本集團確認所有並非持作按公允價值
       (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through             計入損益的債務工具的預期信貸虧損
       profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the                 （「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。預期信貸虧
       contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and all           損是基於根據合約到期的合約現金流
       the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an           量與本集團預期收到的所有現金流量
       approximation of the original effective interest rate. The expected          之間的差額，以原實際利率的近似值
       cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of collateral held          進行折現。預期現金流量將包括出售
       or other credit enhancements that are integral to the contractual            所持抵押品的現金流量或合約條款所
       terms.                                                                       包含的其他信貸升級措施的現金流量。


       General approach                                                             一般方法
       ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for which            預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。對於
       there has not been a significant increase in credit risk since initial       自初始確認以來信用風險沒有大幅增
       recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that result from            加的信用風險敞口，對未來 12 個月內
       default events that are possible within the next 12 months (a                可能發生的違約事件（12個月預期信貸
       12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which there has been           虧損）造成的信貸虧損計提預期信貸虧
       a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss      損撥備。對於自初始確認以來信用風
       allowance is required for credit losses expected over the remaining          險大幅增加的信用風險敞口，無論違
       life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a           約的時間長短（全期預期信貸虧損），
       lifetime ECL).                                                               都需要對風險敞口剩餘年期內預期信
                                                                                    貸虧損計提虧損撥備。


       At the end of the reporting period, the Group assesses whether               於報告期末，本集團評估金融工具的
       the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly        信用風險自初始確認以來是否大幅增
       since initial recognition. When making the assessment, the Group             加。在進行評估時，本集團將報告日
       compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument         期金融工具發生違約的風險與初始確
       as at the reporting date with the risk of a default occurring on             認日期金融工具發生違約的風險進行
       the financial instrument as at the date of initial recognition               比較，並考慮無需付出過多成本或努
       and considers reasonable and supportable information that is                 力即可取得的合理有理據資料，包括
       available without undue cost or effort, including historical and             歷史及前瞻性資料。
       forward-looking information.


       The Group considers a financial asset in default when contractual            當合約付款逾期360天時，本集團將金
       payments are 360 days past due. However, in certain cases, the               融資產視為違約。然而，於若干情況
       Group may also consider a financial asset to be in default when              下，當內部或外部資料表明本集團在
       internal or external information indicates that the Group is unlikely        考慮其擁有的任何信貸升級措施之前
       to receive the outstanding contractual amounts in full before taking         不太可能收到全部未付合約金額時，
       into account any credit enhancements held by the Group.                      本集團亦會將金融資產視為違約。

       Simplified approach                                                             簡化方法
       For trade receivables and contract assets that do not contain a                 對於不包含重大融資組成部分的貿易
       significant financing component or when the Group applies the                   應收款項及合約資產，或當本集團採
       practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a significant                用不調整重大融資組成部分影響的實
       financing component, the Group applies the simplified approach                  際權宜方法時，本集團在計算預期信
       in calculating ECLs. Under the simplified approach, the Group                   貸虧損時採用簡化方法。根據簡化方
       does not track changes in credit risk, but instead recognises a loss            法，本集團不跟蹤信用風險的變化，
       allowance based on lifetime ECLs at each reporting date. The Group              而是根據於各報告日期的全期預期信
       has established a provision matrix that is based on its historical              貸虧損確認虧損撥備。本集團已根據
       credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors specific           其歷史信貸虧損經驗建立了撥備矩
       to the debtors and the economic environment.                                    陣，並對債務人和經濟環境的特定前
                                                                                       瞻性因素進行了調整。


       For trade receivables and contract assets that contain a significant            對於包含重大融資組成部分的貿易應
       financing component and lease receivables, the Group chooses as                 收款項及合約資產以及租賃應收款
       its accounting policy to adopt the simplified approach in calculating           項，本集團選擇採用簡化方法計算預
       ECLs with policies as described above.                                          期信貸虧損，並採用上述政策作為其

       attributable transaction costs.                                                 扣除直接應佔交易成本後確認。

       subsidiary, and interest-bearing bank and other borrowings.                     負債、一家附屬公司的贖回負債以及

     Financial liabilities (continued)                                              金融負債（續）
     Initial recognition and measurement (continued)                                初始確認及計量（續）
     The Group classifies financial liabilities that arise from a supplier          倘供應商融資安排產生的金融負債與
     finance arrangement within trade and bills payables in the                     貿易應付款項的性質及功能相似，本
     statement of financial position if they have a similar nature and              集團會將該等金融負債分類為貿易應
     function to trade payables. This is the case if the supplier finance           收款項及應付票據計入財務狀況表。
     arrangement is part of the working capital used in the Group’s                 倘供應商融資安排為本集團正常營運
     normal operating cycle, the level of security provided is similar              週期中使用的運營資金的一部分，所
     to trade payables and the terms of the liabilities that are part               提供的擔保水平與貿易應付款項相
     of the supply chain finance arrangement are not substantially                  若，且屬於供應鏈融資安排一部分的
     different from the terms of trade payables that are not part of                負債條款與不屬於該安排一部分的貿
     the arrangement. Cash flows related to liabilities arising from                易應付款項的條款並無重大差異，則
     supplier finance arrangements that are classified in trade and bills           情況屬實。於財務狀況表中分類為貿
     payables in the statement of financial position are included in                易應付款項及應付票據的與供應商融
     operating activities in the statement of cash flows. Otherwise, the            資安排產生的負債相關的現金流量，
     financial liabilities are classified in interest-bearing bank and other        計入現金流量表中的經營活動。否
     borrowings in the statement of financial position and the related              則，金融負債於財務狀況表中分類為
     cash flows are included in financing activities in the statement of            計息銀行及其他借款，而相關現金流
     cash flows.                                                                    量計入現金流量表中的融資活動。


     Subsequent measurement                                                         後續計量
     The subsequent measurement of financial liabilities depends on                 金融負債根據以下分類進行後續計量：
     their classification as follows:


     Financial liabilities at amortised cost (trade and other                       按攤銷成本計量的金融負債（貿
     payables, and borrowings)                                                      易及其他應付款項以及借款）
     After initial recognition, trade and other payables, and                       初始確認後，貿易及其他應付款項以
     interest-bearing bank and other borrowings are subsequently                    及計息銀行及其他借款隨後採用實際
     measured at amortised cost, using the effective interest rate                  利率法按攤銷成本計量，除非折現的
     method unless the effect of discounting would be immaterial,                   影響不重大，在這種情況下按成本列
     in which case they are stated at cost. Gains and losses are                    示。終止確認負債時以及通過實際利
     recognised in the statement of profit or loss when the liabilities             率攤銷過程在損益表中確認收益或虧
     are derecognised as well as through the effective interest rate                損。
     amortisation process.


     Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account any discount or            攤銷成本的計算方法是將任何購置折
     premium on acquisition and fees or costs that are an integral part             價或溢價以及作為實際利率不可分割
     of the effective interest rate. The effective interest rate amortisation       的一部分的費用或成本納入考慮。實
     is included in finance costs in the statement of profit or loss.               際利率攤銷計入損益表的財務費用。

     Derecognition of financial liabilities                                      終止確認金融負債
     A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under the         當金融負債的義務被解除或取消或到
     liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                           期時，終止確認該金融負債。


     When an existing financial liability is replaced by another from the        當現有金融負債由同一出借人以實質
     same lender on substantially different terms, or the terms of an            上不同的條款替換為另一金融負債，
     existing liability are substantially modified, such an exchange or          或現有負債的條款被實質性修改時，
     modification is treated as a derecognition of the original liability        這種替換或修改被視為對原負債的終
     and a recognition of a new liability, and the difference between the        止確認及對新負債的確認，其賬面值
     respective carrying amounts is recognised in the statement of profit        之間的差額在損益表中確認。
     or loss.


     Offsetting of financial instruments                                         金融工具的抵銷
     Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the net           當現時有法定可執行權利抵銷已確認
     amount is reported in the statement of financial position if there is       金額，並有意圖按淨額基準結算或同
     a currently enforceable legal right to offset the recognised amounts        時變現資產和結算負債時，金融資產
     and there is an intention to settle on a net basis, or to realise the       與金融負債可相互抵銷，並在財務狀
     assets and settle the liabilities simultaneously.                           況表報告其淨額。

     The derivative financial instrument is initially recognised at fair         衍生金融工具於衍生工具合約訂立當

     reporting period. Change in fair value of the derivative financial          融工具的公允價值變動於損益表確認。
     instrument is recognised in the statement of profit or loss.


     Inventories                                                                 存貨
     Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable value.       存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較低
     Cost is determined on the weighted average basis and, in the                者列賬。成本按加權平均基準釐定，倘
     case of work in progress and finished goods, comprises direct               為在建工程及製成品，則包括直接材

     Net realisable value is based on estimated selling prices less any          變現淨值乃根據估計售價減完成及出售
     estimated costs to be incurred to completion and disposal.                  所產生的任何估計成本計算。

     Income tax                                                                    所得稅
     Income tax comprises current and deferred tax. Income tax relating            所得稅包括當期及遞延稅項。不在損
     to items recognised outside profit or loss is recognised outside              益確認的項目相關的所得稅不計入損

     substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period, taking              詮釋及慣例預期獲稅務部門退回或已
     into consideration interpretations and practices prevailing in the            付予稅務部門的數額計量。
     countries in which the Group operates.


     Deferred tax is provided, using the liability method, on all                  遞延稅項使用負債法，就於報告期末資
     temporary differences at the end of the reporting period between              產及負債的稅基與其作財務申報用途的
     the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying amounts for        賬面值之間所有暫時差額計提撥備。
     financial reporting purposes.

     differences, except:                                                          負債，除非：

         of goodwill or an asset or liability in a transaction that is not               合併交易的商譽或資產或負債所產
         a business combination and, at the time of the transaction,                     生，而在交易時既不影響會計利潤
         affects neither the accounting profit nor taxable profit or                     亦不影響應課稅利潤或虧損，且不
         loss and does not give rise to equal taxable and deductible                     產生相等的應課稅及可抵扣暫時差
         temporary differences; and                                                      額；及

         when the timing of the reversal of the temporary differences                    本集團能夠控制暫時差額撥回的時

     Government grants                                                             政府補助

     compensate, are expensed.


     Where the grant relates to an asset, the fair value is credited to a          倘有關補助與一項資產有關，公允價
     deferred income account and is released to the statement of profit            值計入遞延收入賬，並於相關資產的
     or loss over the expected useful life of the relevant asset by equal          預計可使用年期每年等額分期調撥至
     annual instalments or deducted from the carrying amount of the                損益表，或者從資產的賬面值中減去
     asset and released to the statement of profit or loss by way of a             並通過減少折舊費用的方式調撥至損
     reduced depreciation charge.                                                  益表。


     Where the Group receives government loans granted with no                     倘本集團收到用於產業扶持而發放的

     “Financial liabilities” above. The benefit of the government loans            明。發放的無息或按低於市場利率計

       the discount rate that would be reflected in a separate financing       達成一次單獨的融資交易中反映的折

       inception.

       the amount of consideration is estimated to which the Group will        金額估計為本集團因向客戶轉讓貨品或

       its financial statements to reflect any adjusting events after the            金額以反映報告期後的任何調整事
       reporting period and update the disclosures that relate to those              項，及更新與該等情況有關的披露資
       conditions in light of the new information. For non-adjusting events          料。就報告期後的非調整事項而言，
       after the reporting period, the Group will not change the amounts             本集團不會調整於其財務報表確認的
       recognised in its financial statements, but will disclose the nature of       金額，但會披露非調整事項的性質及
       the non-adjusting events and an estimate of their financial effects,          其財務影響的估計或無法作出有關估
       or a statement that such an estimate cannot be made, if applicable.           計的陳述（如適用）。

  Deferred tax assets                                                             遞延稅項資產
  Deferred tax assets are recognised for unused tax losses to the extent          倘很可能有應課稅利潤可動用虧損抵銷，
  that it is probable that taxable profit will be available against which the     則就未動用稅項虧損確認遞延稅項資產。
  losses can be utilised. Significant management judgement is required            管理層於釐定可予以確認的遞延稅項資產
  to determine the amount of deferred tax assets that can be recognised,          數額時，須根據可能出現未來應課稅利潤
  based upon the likely timing and the level of future taxable profits,           的時間及數額，以及未來稅務規劃策略作
  together with future tax planning strategies.                                   出重大判斷。


  The Group has tax losses of RMB2,871,711,000 (2023: RMB2,429,235,000)           本集團產生結轉稅項虧損人民幣2,871,711,000
  carried forward. These losses related to subsidiaries that have a history of    元（2023年：人民幣2,429,235,000元）
                                                                                                           。該等虧

   Management has determined the operating segment based on the                管理層已根據本集團的主要經營決策者所
   information reviewed by the Group’s chief operating decision maker,         審閱資料釐定經營分部，主要經營決策者
   which is responsible for allocating resources and assessing performance     負責分配資源及評估經營分部表現，已被
   of the operating segment. The chief operating decision maker has been       定為本公司的執行董事。
   identified as the executive directors of the Company.


   Management monitors the results of the Group’s operating segment            管理層分開監察本集團經營分部的業績，
   separately for the purpose of making decisions about resource allocation    以就資源分配及表現評估方面作出決定，
   and performance assessment, focuses on the operating results of             專注於本集團整體的經營業績，乃由於
   the Group as a whole as the Group’s resources are integrated and no         本集團的資源統一且並無獨立經營分部資
   discrete operating segment information is available. Accordingly, no        料。因此，並無呈列經營分部的進一步資
   further information about the operating segment is presented.               料。


   Geographical information                                                    地區資料

   Mainland China. The geographical location of customers is based on the      內地。客戶的地理位置基於客戶經營所在
   location at which the customers operate, and most of the revenue of the     位置，於年內，本集團的大部分收入來自
   Group was derived from operations in Mainland China during the year.        中國內地的業務。

4. O P E R A T I N G S E G M E N T I N F O R M A T I O N                         4. 經營分部資料（續）
   (continued)
   Information about major customers (continued)                                  有關主要客戶的資料（續）
   Seasonality of operations                                                      業務的季節性
   The Group is a supplier of fuel cell systems in fuel cell vehicle industry.    本集團為燃料電池汽車行業的燃料電池系
   Higher revenues and operating profits are usually expected in the              統供應商。預計一般於下半年（尤其是第四
   second half of the year, especially in the fourth quarter of the year.         季度）會有更高的收入及經營利潤。第四季
   Higher sales during the fourth quarter are mainly attributed to the fact       度銷量較高主要是由於中國氫燃料電池汽
   that policies and awards for fuel cell vehicles are typically announced or     車行業仍處於早期發展階段，在很大程度
   updated during the first half of each year by relevant local governments,      上受政府補貼政策的影響，相關地方政府
   as China’s hydrogen fuel cell vehicle industry is still in the early stage     一般在每年的上半年發佈或修訂燃料電池
   of development and is highly affected by the government subsidy                汽車的政策及獎勵，而客戶將根據最新的
   policies and customers will determine their purchase plan under the            政府補貼政策確定其購買計劃，導致客戶
   updated government subsidy policies, resulting in the concentration of         的備貨需求集中在下半年。
   customers’ stocking demand in the second half of the year.

10. INCOME TAX                                                                10. 所得稅
  The Group is subject to income tax on an entity basis on profits arising      本集團須以實體為單位就在本集團成員公
  in or derived from the jurisdictions in which members of the Group are        司住所及經營所在的司法管轄區產生或來
  domiciled and operate.                                                        自相關司法管轄區的利潤繳納所得稅。


  Mainland China                                                                中國內地
  The subsidiaries incorporated in Mainland China are subject to tax at the     中國內地註冊成立的附屬公司須就根據
  statutory rate of 25% on the taxable profits determined in accordance         2008年1月1日生效的《中華人民共和國企業
  with the PRC Corporate Income Tax Law which became effective on 1             所得稅法》確定的應課稅利潤按25%的法定
  January 2008, except for those subject to tax preferential policy set out     稅率繳稅，但下文所載適用稅收優惠政策
  below:                                                                        的附屬公司除外：


  REFIRE Technology, Shanghai PANDO. and Shanghai Unilia were granted           重塑科技、上海磐動及上海韻量獲授高新
  the qualification of High and New Technology Enterprises (“HNTE”).            技術企業（「高新技術企業」）資格。因此，
  Accordingly, the subsidiaries were entitled to a preferential corporate       這些附屬公司有權於年內享有15%的優惠企
  income tax rate of 15% during the year.                                       業所得稅稅率。


  Certain subsidiaries of the Group have applied the Small-Scaled Minimal       本集團的若干附屬公司已適用中國國家稅
  Profit Corporate Income Tax Preferential Policy announced by the PRC’s        務總局公佈的小型微利企業所得稅優惠政
  State Administration of Taxation. For small-scale low-profit enterprises,     策。就小型微利企業而言，減按25%的稅率
  the policy of calculating the taxable income at a reduced rate of 25%         計算應納稅所得額及按20%的稅率繳納企業
  and levying corporate income tax at a rate of 20% will continue to be in      所得稅的政策將持續生效至2027 年12 月31
  effect until December 31, 2027.                                               日。


  Hong Kong                                                                     中國香港
  The subsidiary incorporated in Hong Kong is subject to Hong Kong              就於年內在香港產生的估計應課稅利潤而
  profits tax at the rate of 8.25% for taxable income not exceeding             言，在香港註冊成立的附屬公司須就不超
  HKD2,000,000, and 16.5% for taxable income exceeding HKD2,000,000 on          過2 百萬港元的應評稅利潤按8.25%及就應
  any estimated assessable profits arising in Hong Kong during the year.        評稅利潤中超過2 百萬港元的部分按16.5%
  No provision for Hong Kong profits tax has been made as the Group had         的稅率繳納香港利得稅。由於本集團於年
  no assessable profits derived from or earned in Hong Kong during the          內並無源自香港或在香港賺取的應課稅利
  year.                                                                         潤，故並無作出香港利得稅撥備。

   the Group is restricted from assigning and subleasing the leased assets     集團一般被限制將租賃資產轉讓及轉租到
   outside the Group.                                                          本集團之外。

   strict control over its outstanding receivables and has a credit control      力嚴格控制其未結應收款項，並設有信用
   department to minimise credit risk.                                           控制部門以盡量降低信用風險。

20. TRADE AND BILLS RECEIVABLES AND                                           20. 貿易應收款項及應收票據以及
    CONTRACT ASSETS (continued)                                                   合約資產（續）
   (a) Non-current trade receivables represented receivables                   (a) 非 流 動 貿 易 應 收 款 項 為 應 收 信
       from customers who signed contracts with a credit                           用期超過 1 年的已訂約客戶的款
       period of more than 1 year. (continued)                                     項。（續）
       The Group applies the simplified approach in calculating ECLs             本集團在計算貿易應收款項、商業承
       for trade receivables, commercial acceptance bills and contract           兌匯票及合約資產的預期信貸虧損時
       assets. Trade receivable, commercial acceptance bills and contract        採用簡化方法。與其他客戶無類似信
       assets relating to customers not sharing similar credit risk with         用風險的客戶（如存在已知財務困難或
       others are assessed individually for impairment allowance, for            收回款項重大存疑的客戶）有關的貿易
       instance, customers with known financial difficulties or significant      應收款項、商業承兌匯票及合約資產
       doubt on collection. The remaining trade receivables, commercial          會進行單項評估減值撥備。其餘貿易
       acceptance bills and contract assets are grouped and collectively         應收款項、商業承兌匯票及合約資產
       assessed for impairment allowance. Under the collective approach,         予以分組並進行組合評估減值撥備。
       an impairment analysis is performed at each reporting date                根據組合法，於各報告日期採用撥備
       using a provision matrix to measure expected credit losses. The           矩陣進行減值分析，以計量預期信貸
       provision rates are based on past due information for grouping of         虧損。撥備率乃基於具有類似損失模
       customers that have similar loss patterns. The calculation reflects       式的客戶組別的逾期資料計算。該計
       the probability-weighted outcome, the time value of money and             算反映概率加權結果、貨幣時間值及
       reasonable and supportable information that is available at the           於報告日期可得的有關過往事件、當
       reporting date about past events, current conditions and forecasts        前狀況及未來經濟狀況預測的合理有
       of future economic conditions. Generally, trade receivables are           理據資料。一般而言，貿易應收款項
       written off if past due for more than five years and are not subject      若逾期超過五年則予以撇銷且不受強
       to enforcement activity. During 2024, the increase in impairment          制執行活動所規限。於 2024 年，貿易
       losses on trade receivables, commercial acceptance bills and              應收款項、商業承兌匯票及合約資產
       contract assets was mainly due to the rise in the expected loss           減值損失增加主要是由於預期虧損撥
       provision matrix along with the ageing migration.                         備矩陣隨著賬齡遷徙而上升。


       Set out below is the information about the credit risk exposure on        下表載列使用撥備矩陣計算的本集團
       the Group’s trade receivables, commercial acceptance bills and            貿易應收款項、商業承兌匯票及合約
       contract assets using a provision matrix:                                 資產面臨的信用風險資料：

28. REDEMPTION LIABILITIES OF A SUBSIDIARY                                     28. 一家附屬公司的贖回負債
  In June 2024, one of the Group’s subsidiaries, Shanghai PANDO, obtained       於2024年6月，本集團的其中一家附屬公司
  RMB10,000,000 capital investment from an independent investor (the            上海磐動獲得一名獨立投資者（「該股東」）
  “Shareholder”). The key terms of the preferred rights granted to the          的資本投資人民幣 10,000,000 元。授予投
  investor are summarised as follows: Upon occurrence of the following          資者的優先權的主要條款概述如下：於以
  events, the capital investment shall be redeemable at the option of the       下事件發生後，該股東可選擇贖回資本投
  Shareholder: 1) Shanghai PANDO fails to achieve a Qualified Initial Public    資：1)上海磐動於2027年12月31日前未完成
  Offering (“Qualified IPO”) by December 31, 2027; 2) The Group stops           合資格首次公開發售（「合資格首次公開發
  providing liquidity support to Shanghai PANDO.                                售」）；2)本集團停止向上海磐動提供流動資
                                                                                金支持。


  The redemption amount is calculated as the higher of (i) the original         贖回金額按以下較高者計算：(i)投資者的原
  investment principal from the investor, plus an annual rate of 8% of the      始投資本金，加上自投資者向上海磐動支
  original investment principal for a period of time commencing from            付增資價款之日起至投資者收取回購義務
  the date when the investor pay the capital increase price to Shanghai         人支付的全部回購價款（扣除已收取的現金
  PANDO to the date when the investor receives the full repurchase price        股息）之日止期間（按一個曆年365 天計算）
  paid by the repurchase obligor (calculated as 365 days in a calendar          原始投資本金8%的年利率；(ii)於投資者提
  year), net of the cash dividends already received; (ii) the audited net       出回購要求時本公司的經審核淨資產（綜合
  assets of the company at the time of the investor’s repurchase request        基準）乘以投資者的持股比率。
  (consolidated basis) multiplied by the investor’s shareholding ratio.


  The redemption rights granted to the investor constitute as Shanghai          授予投資者的贖回權構成上海磐動回購其
  PANDO’s obligations to repurchase its own equity instruments. This            自身權益工具的責任。該責任確認為贖回
  obligation was recognised as redemption liabilities which are initially       負債，贖回負債初步按公允價值（倘該等權
  measured at fair value (representing the present value of the expected        利獲投資者行使，則指結算有關責任的預
  cash flows for settling the related obligations if these rights are           計現金流量現值）計量，其後按攤銷成本計
  exercised by the investors) and subsequently measured at amortised            量。上海磐動採用8%的贖回折現率釐定贖
  cost. Shanghai PANDO applied a redemption discount rate of 8% to              回負債的初始確認金額。
  determine the initial recognition amount of the redemption liabilities.

33. RESERVES                                                                      33. 儲備
   The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements therein for the            本集團的儲備金額及儲備於本年度及過往
   current and prior years are presented in the consolidated statement of           年度的變動呈列於財務報表第144 至146 頁
   changes in equity on pages 144 to 146 of the financial statements.               綜合權益變動表。


   (i) Share premium and other reserve                                              (i) 股份溢價及其他儲備
       The share premium and other reserve of the Group mainly                        本集團的股份溢價及其他儲備主要指
       represents the premium in issuing shares and the premium in                    發行股份的溢價及收購非控股權益的
       acquisition of non-controlling interest.                                       溢價。


   (ii) Statutory surplus reserve                                                   (ii) 法定盈餘儲備
       In accordance with the Company Law of the PRC, certain                         根據《中國公司法》，本集團的若干附
       subsidiaries of the Group which are domestic enterprises are                   屬公司（為境內企業）須將根據相關中
       required to allocate 10% of their profit after tax, as determined in           國會計準則釐定其稅後利潤的 10% 轉
       accordance with the relevant PRC accounting standards, to their                撥至其各自的法定盈餘儲備，直至該
       respective statutory surplus reserves until the reserves reach 50% of          儲備達至其各自註冊資本的50%。根據
       their respective registered capital. Subject to certain restrictions set       《中國公司法》載列的若干限制，部分
       out in the Company Law of the PRC, part of the statutory surplus               法定盈餘儲備可轉換為股本，惟於資
       reserve may be converted to share capital, provided that the                   本化後的剩餘結餘不少於註冊資本的
       remaining balance after the capitalisation is not less than 25% of             25%。
       the registered capital.


   (iii) Exchange fluctuation reserve                                               (iii) 外匯波動儲備
       The exchange fluctuation reserve represents exchange differences               外匯波動儲備指換算功能貨幣有別於
       arising from the translation of the financial statements of foreign            本集團呈列貨幣的海外業務的財務報
       operations whose functional currencies are different from the                  表所產生之匯兌差額。
       Group’s presentation currency.


   (iv) Share-based payment reserve                                                 (iv) 以股份為基礎的付款儲備
       The share-based payment reserve represents the equity-settled                  以股份為基礎的付款儲備指財務報表
       share awards as set out in note 32 to the financial statements.                附註32所載以權益結算的股份獎勵。

41. TRANSFERS OF FINANCIAL ASSETS                                            41. 金融資產轉移
  Transferred financial assets that are derecognised in their                 全部終止確認的已轉移金融資產
  entirety
  During the year, certain subsidiaries of the Group, endorsed certain        於年內，本集團的若干附屬公司向其若干
  bills receivable accepted by banks (the “Derecognised Bills”) to certain    供應商背書獲銀行承兌的若干應收票據
  of their suppliers in order to settle the trade payables due to such        （「已終止確認票據」），以結清應付該等供
  suppliers with carrying amounts in aggregate of RMB26,910,374 (2023:        應商貿易款項，已終止確認票據的賬面總
  nil). The Derecognised Bills had a maturity of 1 to 12 months at the end    值為人民幣26,910,374元（2023年：零）。已
  of the year. In accordance with the Law of Negotiable Instruments, the      終止確認票據於年末起 1 至 12 個月到期。
  holders of the Derecognised Bills may exercise the right of recourse        根據《票據法》，已終止確認票據持有人可
  against any, several or all of the persons, including the Group, liable     以不按照先後順序對包括本集團在內的一
  for the Derecognised Bills regardless of the order of precedence            名、多名或全部已終止確認票據債務人行
  (the “Continuing Involvement”). In the opinion of management, the           使 追 索 權（「 繼 續 涉 入 」）。 管 理 層 認 為 ，
  Group has transferred substantially all the risks and rewards relating      本集團已轉移有關已終止確認票據的絕大
  to the Derecognised Bills. Accordingly, it has derecognised the full        部分風險及回報，故其已悉數終止確認已
  carrying amounts of the Derecognised Bills and the associated trade         終止確認票據及相關貿易應付款項的賬面
  payables. The maximum exposure to loss from the Group’s Continuing          值。本集團繼續涉入已終止確認票據及購
  Involvement in the Derecognised Bills and the undiscounted cash             回該等已終止確認票據的未折現現金流量
  flows to repurchase these Derecognised Bills is equal to their carrying     所面臨的最大損失相等於其賬面值。管理
  amounts. In the opinion of management, the fair values of the Group’s       層認為，本集團繼續涉入已終止確認票據
  Continuing Involvement in the Derecognised Bills are not significant.       的公允價值並不重大。

    FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                                             值層級
   Management has assessed that the fair values of cash and cash                   管理層評估得出，現金及現金等價物、受
   equivalents, restricted cash, time deposits, trade and bills receivables,       限制現金、定期存款、貿易應收款項及應
   financial assets included in prepayments, other receivables and other           收票據、計入預付款項、其他應收款項及
   assets, trade and bills payables, financial liabilities included in other       其他資產的金融資產、貿易應付款項及應
   payables and accruals, lease liabilities and short-term interest-bearing        付票據、計入其他應付款項及應計費用的
   bank and other borrowings approximate to their carrying amounts                 金融負債、租賃負債及短期計息銀行及其
   largely due to the short term maturities of these instruments. The fair         他借款的公允價值與其賬面值相若，主要
   value of the non-current portion of interest-bearing bank and other             是由於這些工具的到期日較短。計息銀行
   borrowings approximates to their carrying amounts mainly due to the             及其他借款的非流動部分公允價值與其賬
   floating interest rate.                                                         面值相若，主要由於浮動利率所致。


   The Group’s finance department headed by the finance manager is                 本集團財務經理主管的財務部負責制定金
   responsible for determining the policies and procedures for the fair            融工具公允價值計量的政策及程序。財務
   value measurement of financial instruments. The finance manager                 經理直接向財務負責人報告。於各報告日
   reports directly to the chief financial officer. At each reporting date, the    期，財務部分析金融工具的價值變動，並
   finance department analyses the movements in the values of financial            確定估值時所應用的主要輸入值。估值由
   instruments and determines the major inputs applied in the valuation.           財務負責人審閱和批准。本集團每年兩次
   The valuation is reviewed and approved by the chief financial officer. The      與審計委員會對估值流程及結果進行討論
   valuation process and results are discussed with the audit committee            以供中期及年度財務報告之用。
   twice a year for interim and annual financial reporting.

42. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY OF                                     42. 金融工具的公允價值及公允價
    FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (continued)                                              值層級（續）
  The fair values of unlisted equity investments designated at fair value       指定按公允價值計入其他全面收益的非上
  through other comprehensive income and unlisted investments                   市股權投資及按公允價值計入損益計量的
  measured at fair value through profit or loss have been estimated using       非上市股權投資的公允價值，均使用基於
  a market-based valuation technique based on assumptions that are not          市 場 的 估 值 技 術（基 於 並 非 以 可 觀 察 市 價
  supported by observable market prices or rates. The valuation requires        或費率作為支撐的假設）而進行估算。估值
  the directors to determine comparable public companies (peers) based          要求董事根據行業、規模、槓桿作用及策
  on industry, size, leverage and strategy, and to calculate an appropriate     略釐定可比較公眾公司（同業），並就每一
  price multiple, such as price to book (“P/B”) multiple and price to sales     識別的可比較公司計算適當的價格倍數，
  (“P/S”) multiple for each comparable company identified. The multiple is      如市賬率（「市賬率」）倍數及市銷率（「市銷
  calculated by dividing the enterprise value of the comparable company         率」）倍數。該倍數是按可比較公司的企業
  by an earnings measure. The trading multiple is then discounted               價值除以盈利計量值計算。交易倍數其後
  for considerations such as illiquidity and size differences between           則根據公司特定事實及情況就可比較公司
  the comparable companies based on company specific facts and                  之間的非流動性及規模差異等考慮因素予
  circumstances. The discounted multiple is applied to the corresponding        以折現。經折現倍數應用於非上市股權投
  earnings measure of the unlisted equity investments to measure the            資的相應盈利計量以計量公允價值。董事
  fair value. The directors believe that the estimated fair values resulting    認為，記錄在綜合財務狀況表的由估值技
  from the valuation technique, which are recorded in the consolidated          術產生的估算公允價值和記錄在其他全面
  statement of financial position, and the related changes in fair values,      收益的公允價值相關變動屬合理，並認為
  which are recorded in other comprehensive income, are reasonable, and         該等項目為在報告期末最為適當的價值。
  that they were the most appropriate values at the end of the reporting
  period.


  The Group has bank acceptance notes issued by banks in Mainland               本集團持有中國內地銀行所簽發按公允
  China measured at fair value through other comprehensive income.              價值計入其他全面收益計量的銀行承兌
  The Group has estimated the fair value of these bank acceptance notes         匯票。本集團已採用折現現金流量估值模
  by using a discounted cash flow valuation model based on the market           型，根據具有類似條款及風險的工具的市
  interest rates of instruments with similar terms and risks.                   場利率估計該等銀行承兌匯票的公允價值。


  For the fair value of the unlisted equity investments designated at           對於指定按公允價值計入其他全面收益的
  fair value through other comprehensive income, management has                 非上市股權投資的公允價值，管理層估計
  estimated the potential effect of using reasonably possible alternatives      使用合理可能的替代數據作為估值模型的
  as inputs to the valuation model.                                             輸入值的潛在影響。

43. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                                           43. 金融風險管理目標及政策
    AND POLICIES
  The Group’s principal financial instruments, comprise bank loans, other           本集團的主要金融工具包括銀行貸款、其
  interest-bearing borrowings, and cash. The main purpose of these                  他計息借款以及現金。這些金融工具的主
  financial instruments is to raise finance for the Group’s operations. The         要目的是為本集團的經營籌集資金。本集
  Group has various other financial assets and liabilities such as trade and        團擁有多項其他金融資產及負債，如經營
  bills receivables and trade and bills payables, which arise directly from its     中直接產生的貿易應收款項及應收票據以
  operations.                                                                       及貿易應付款項及應付票據。


  The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments are interest        本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為利率風
  rate risk, foreign currency risk, credit risk and liquidity risk. The board of    險、外匯風險、信用風險及流動性風險。
  directors reviews and agrees policies for managing each of these risks            董事會審閱及協定管理這些風險中每項風
  and they are summarised below.                                                    險的政策，有關風險概述於下文。


  Interest rate risk                                                                利率風險
  The Group’s exposure to the risk of changes in market interest rates              本集團面臨的市場利率變動風險主要與本
  relates primarily to the Group’s long term debt obligations with a                集團以浮動利率計息的長期債務責任有
  floating interest rate. The Group’s policy is to manage its interest cost         關。本集團的政策是採用固定及可變利率
  using a mix of fixed and variable rate debts.                                     債務組合管理其利息成本。


  As at December 31, 2024, if the interest rate of bank and other                   於2024 年12 月31 日，倘銀行及其他借款的
  borrowings had increased/decreased by 50 basis points and all other               利率上升╱下跌 50 個基點且所有其他變量
  variables were held constant, the loss before tax of the Group, through           保持不變，本集團的除稅前虧損通過對浮
  the impact on floating rate borrowings, would have increased/decreased            動利率借款的影響將增加╱減少約人民幣
  by approximately RMB3,776,000 (2023: RMB3,067,000).                               3,776,000元（2023年：人民幣3,067,000元）。

   Foreign currency risk                                                           外匯風險
   The Group has transactional currency exposures. Such exposures arise            本集團面臨交易貨幣風險。有關風險來自

   It is the Group’s policy that all customers who wish to trade on credit         行交易。本集團的政策是所有欲按信用條
   terms are subject to credit verification procedures. In addition, receivable    款進行交易的客戶均須通過信用核查程
   balances are monitored on an ongoing basis. For transactions that are           序。此外，本集團持續監控應收款項結
   not denominated in the functional currency of the relevant operating            餘。對於並非以相關經營單位的功能貨幣
   unit, the Group does not offer credit terms without specific verification       計值的交易，在未通過特別核查程序的情
   procedures.                                                                     況下，本集團不會提供信用條款。

43. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                                        43. 金融風險管理目標及政策（續）
    AND POLICIES (continued)
  Liquidity risk                                                                 流動性風險
  The Group monitors and maintains a level of cash and cash equivalents          本集團監控現金及現金等價物並將其維持
  deemed adequate by the management of the Group to finance the                  在本集團管理層認為充足的水平，以為經
  operations and mitigate the effects of fluctuations of cash flows.             營撥資並削弱現金流量波動的影響。


  The Group’s objective is to maintain a balance between continuity of           本集團的目標是通過採用計息銀行及其他
  funding and flexibility through the use of interest-bearing bank and           借款以及租賃負債在資金持續性與靈活性
  other borrowings and lease liabilities.                                        之間維持平衡。


  Due to the Group’s supplier finance arrangements, the relevant trade           由於本集團的供應商融資安排，相關貿易
  payables are due to a single counterparty rather than individual               應付款項為應付單一交易對手方而非個別
  suppliers. This results in the Group being required to settle a significant    供應商的款項。這導致本集團須與單一交
  amount with a single counterparty, rather than less significant amounts        易對手方結算一大筆款項，而非與數名供
  with a number of suppliers. However, the Group’s payment terms for             應商結算金額較少的款項。然而，該等安
  trade payables covered by the arrangements are either identical to             排涵蓋的本集團貿易應付款項的付款期與
  the payment terms for other trade payables or extended to not more             其他貿易應付款項的付款期相同或延長至
  than 180 days. Management does not consider the supplier finance               不超過180天。鑒於付款期未大幅延長，管
  arrangements to result in excessive concentrations of liquidity risk           理層認為供應商融資安排不會導致流動性
  given the payment terms are not significantly extended. Details of the         風險過度集中。該等安排的詳情披露於財
  arrangements are disclosed in note 24 to the financial statements.             務報表附註24。


  The maturity profile of the Group’s financial liabilities as at the end of     本集團於報告期末基於已訂合約未折現付
  the reporting period, based on the contractual undiscounted payments,          款的金融負債的到期情況如下：

43. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                                       43. 金融風險管理目標及政策（續）
    AND POLICIES (continued)
  Capital management                                                            資本管理
  The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to               本集團資本管理的主要目標是保障本集團
  safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern and to           持續經營並維持良好資本比率的能力，以
  maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its business and          支持其業務並最大化股東價值。
  maximise shareholders’ value.


  The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments to it           本集團管理其資本結構，並根據經濟狀況
  in light of changes in economic conditions. To maintain or adjust the         的變化對其進行調整。為維持或調整資本
  capital structure, the Group may return capital to shareholders or issue      結構，本集團或會退還股東資本或發行新
  new shares.                                                                   股。


  The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is net debt           本 集 團 採 用 資 產 負 債 比 率（按 債 務 淨 額 除
  divided by total capital plus net debt. The Group includes, within            以總資本再加上債務淨額計算）監控資本。
  net debt, interest-bearing bank and other borrowings, trade and bills         本集團的債務淨額包括計息銀行及其他借
  payables, other payables and accruals and lease liabilities, less cash and    款、貿易應付款項及應付票據、其他應付
  cash equivalents. Capital includes equity attributable to the owners of       款項及應計費用及租賃負債，減現金及現
  the parent.                                                                   金等價物。資本包括母公司擁有人應佔權
                                                                                益。


  The Group has established supplier finance arrangements to manage its         本集團已設立供應商融資安排以管理其營
  working capital, details of which are included in note 24 to the financial    運資金，其詳情載於財務報表附註24。
  statements.
